import {
  getAuthenticatedProjects,
  getAuthenticatedProject,
  createAuthenticatedProject,
  updateAuthenticatedProject,
  setAuthenticatedProjectArchiveStatus,
  deleteAuthenticatedProject,
} from "../services/Service/projectService.js";

function setPrivateHeaders(response) {
  response.set({
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private",
    Pragma: "no-cache",
    Expires: "0",
  });
}

function projectRequestError(message) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.name = "ProjectRequestError";
  error.code = "PROJECT_REQUEST_INVALID";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = message;
  return error;
}

function requireObject(value, name) {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
    throw projectRequestError(`${name} must be a JSON object.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function requireFields(body, allowedFields) {
  const input = requireObject(body, "Request body");

  const unexpected = Object.keys(input).filter(
    (field) => !allowedFields.includes(field),
  );

  if (unexpected.length > 0) {
    throw projectRequestError(
      `Unexpected request fields: ${unexpected.join(", ")}.`,
    );
  }

  return input;
}

export async function listMyProjects(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const projects = await getAuthenticatedProjects({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Projects retrieved successfully.",
      data: { projects },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function getMyProject(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const project = await getAuthenticatedProject({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project retrieved successfully.",
      data: { project },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function createMyProject(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const project = await createAuthenticatedProject({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      project: request.body,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(201).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project created successfully.",
      data: { project },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function updateMyProject(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const body = requireFields(request.body, ["project", "rowVersion"]);

    const project = await updateAuthenticatedProject({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      project: body.project,
      rowVersion: body.rowVersion,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project updated successfully.",
      data: { project },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function setMyProjectArchiveStatus(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const body = requireFields(request.body, ["archived", "rowVersion"]);

    const project = await setAuthenticatedProjectArchiveStatus({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      archived: body.archived,
      rowVersion: body.rowVersion,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: body.archived
        ? "Project archived successfully."
        : "Project restored successfully.",
      data: { project },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function deleteMyProject(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const body = requireFields(request.body, ["rowVersion"]);

    const result = await deleteAuthenticatedProject({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      rowVersion: body.rowVersion,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project deleted successfully.",
      data: result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

import fs from "node:fs";
import { promises as fsPromises } from "node:fs";

import {
  registerProjectDocument,
  listAuthenticatedProjectDocuments,
  getAuthenticatedProjectDocument,
  deleteAuthenticatedProjectDocument,
} from "../services/Service/projectDocumentService.js";

import {
  resolveOwnedProjectDocumentPath,
  assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory,
} from "../services/Storage/projectDocumentStorageService.js";

function setPrivateHeaders(response) {
  response.set({
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private",
    Pragma: "no-cache",
    Expires: "0",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
  });
}

function createNotFoundError() {
  const error = new Error("Project document not found.");
  error.status = 404;
  error.code = "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND";
  error.publicMessage = error.message;
  return error;
}

export async function uploadMyProjectDocument(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const document = await registerProjectDocument({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      uploadedFile: request.projectDocumentFile,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(201).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project document uploaded successfully.",
      data: { document },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function listMyProjectDocuments(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const documents = await listAuthenticatedProjectDocuments({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project documents retrieved successfully.",
      data: { documents },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function getMyProjectDocument(request, response, next) {
  let fileHandle;

  try {
    const userId = request.auth.userId;
    const projectId = request.params.projectId;
    const documentId = request.params.documentId;

    const document = await getAuthenticatedProjectDocument({
      userId,
      projectId,
      documentId,
    });

    const filePath = resolveOwnedProjectDocumentPath(
      userId,
      projectId,
      document.storageKey,
    );

    await assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const pathStats = await fsPromises.lstat(filePath);

    if (pathStats.isSymbolicLink() || !pathStats.isFile()) {
      throw createNotFoundError();
    }

    const flags =
      fs.constants.O_RDONLY | (fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0);

    fileHandle = await fsPromises.open(filePath, flags);

    const stats = await fileHandle.stat();

    if (!stats.isFile() || stats.size !== document.fileSize) {
      throw createNotFoundError();
    }

    // Prevent CR/LF and header parameter injection.
    const safeName = String(document.originalFileName || "document")
      .replace(/[\r\n"\\]/g, "_")
      .replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, "_")
      .slice(0, 200);

    const encodedName = encodeURIComponent(
      document.originalFileName || "document",
    );

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    response.set({
      "Content-Type": document.mimeType,
      "Content-Length": String(stats.size),
      "Content-Disposition":
        `attachment; filename="${safeName}"; filename*=UTF-8''${encodedName}`,
      "Accept-Ranges": "none",
    });

    const stream = fileHandle.createReadStream({
      autoClose: true,
    });

    fileHandle = null;

    stream.on("error", (error) => {
      if (!response.headersSent) {
        next(error);
      } else {
        response.destroy(error);
      }
    });

    stream.pipe(response);
  } catch (error) {
    if (fileHandle) {
      await fileHandle.close().catch(() => {});
    }

    if (error.code === "ENOENT") {
      return next(createNotFoundError());
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function deleteMyProjectDocument(request, response, next) {
  try {
    await deleteAuthenticatedProjectDocument({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      documentId: request.params.documentId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(204).end();
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

import fs from "node:fs";
import { promises as fsPromises } from "node:fs";

import {
  registerProjectMedia,
  listAuthenticatedProjectMedia,
  getAuthenticatedProjectMedia,
  deleteAuthenticatedProjectMedia,
} from "../services/Service/projectMediaService.js";

import {
  resolveOwnedProjectMediaPath,
  assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory,
} from "../services/Storage/projectMediaStorageService.js";

function setPrivateHeaders(response) {
  response.set({
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private",
    Pragma: "no-cache",
    Expires: "0",
  });
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Upload Project Media
 * =========================================
 *
 * Authentication, ownership, upload, and
 * file validation must run before this
 * controller.
 *
 * File cleanup is handled by the validation
 * middleware for rejected uploads.
 *
 * Do not delete an uploaded file after an
 * uncertain database registration outcome.
 */

export async function uploadMyProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const media = await registerProjectMedia({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      uploadedFile: request.projectMediaFile,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(201).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project media uploaded successfully.",
      data: { media },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

/*
 * GET /api/projects/:projectId/media
 */

export async function listMyProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const media = await listAuthenticatedProjectMedia({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(200).json({
      status: "success",
      message: "Project media retrieved successfully.",
      data: { media },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Serve Project Media
 * =========================================
 *
 * GET /api/projects/:projectId/media/:mediaId
 *
 * Authentication and Project ownership
 * must be verified by the route middleware.
 */

export async function getMyProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  let fileHandle;

  try {
    const userId = request.auth.userId;
    const projectId = request.params.projectId;
    const mediaId = request.params.mediaId;

    /*
     * Retrieve authorized media metadata.
     */

    const media = await getAuthenticatedProjectMedia({
      userId,
      projectId,
      mediaId,
    });

    /*
     * Resolve the file path using the
     * existing ownership-aware storage service.
     */

    const filePath = resolveOwnedProjectMediaPath(
      userId,
      projectId,
      media.storageKey,
    );

    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId);

    /*
     * Open the file without following a
     * symbolic link on supported platforms.
     */

    /*
     * Reject symbolic links and reparse-point-like
     * targets before opening the file.
     *
     * On Windows, O_NOFOLLOW is unavailable.
     */

    const pathStats = await fsPromises.lstat(filePath);

    if (pathStats.isSymbolicLink() || !pathStats.isFile()) {
      const error = new Error("Project media not found.");

      error.status = 404;
      error.code = "PROJECT_MEDIA_NOT_FOUND";
      error.publicMessage = error.message;

      throw error;
    }

    /*
     * Open the file using O_NOFOLLOW where supported.
     */

    const flags = fs.constants.O_RDONLY | (fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0);

    fileHandle = await fsPromises.open(filePath, flags);

    const stats = await fileHandle.stat();

    if (!stats.isFile()) {
      const error = new Error("Project media is not a regular file.");
      error.status = 404;
      error.code = "PROJECT_MEDIA_NOT_FOUND";
      error.publicMessage = "Project media not found.";
      throw error;
    }

    /*
     * Set private response headers.
     */

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    response.set({
      "Content-Type": media.mimeType,
      "Content-Length": String(stats.size),
      "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
      "Content-Disposition": "inline",
      "Accept-Ranges": "none",
    });

    /*
     * Stream from the already-open file
     * descriptor rather than reopening
     * the path.
     */

    const stream = fileHandle.createReadStream({
      autoClose: true,
    });

    fileHandle = null;

    stream.on("error", (error) => {
      if (!response.headersSent) {
        next(error);
      } else {
        response.destroy(error);
      }
    });

    stream.pipe(response);
  } catch (error) {
    if (fileHandle) {
      await fileHandle.close().catch(() => {});
    }

    if (error.code === "ENOENT") {
      const notFound = new Error("Project media not found.");
      notFound.status = 404;
      notFound.code = "PROJECT_MEDIA_NOT_FOUND";
      notFound.publicMessage = notFound.message;
      return next(notFound);
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Delete My Project Media
 * =========================================
 */

export async function deleteMyProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  try {
    await deleteAuthenticatedProjectMedia({
      userId: request.auth.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
      mediaId: request.params.mediaId,
    });

    setPrivateHeaders(response);

    return response.status(204).end();
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\middleware\project\projectDocumentValidationMiddleware.js

import yauzl from "yauzl";

import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { fileTypeFromFile } from "file-type";

import {
  ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_TYPES,
  MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE,
} from "./projectDocumentUploadMiddleware.js";

import {
  assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory,
  getProjectDocumentDirectory,
} from "../../services/Storage/projectDocumentStorageService.js";

function createValidationError(message, code, status = 400) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProjectDocumentValidationError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = message;

  return error;
}

function getAuthorizedFilePath(request) {
  const userId = String(request.auth?.userId ?? "").trim();
  const projectId = String(request.params?.projectId ?? "").trim();
  const authorizedProjectId = String(
    request.authorizedProject?.id ?? "",
  ).trim();

  if (!userId || !projectId || authorizedProjectId !== projectId) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project document validation is not authorized.",
      "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_VALIDATION_UNAUTHORIZED",
      403,
    );
  }

  if (!request.file?.path) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Please select a Project document to upload.",
      "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_FILE_REQUIRED",
    );
  }

  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const uploadedPath = path.resolve(request.file.path);

  if (path.dirname(uploadedPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project document file location is invalid.",
      "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_INVALID_FILE_LOCATION",
      403,
    );
  }

  return uploadedPath;
}

async function removeRejectedFile(userId, projectId, filePath) {
  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const targetPath = path.resolve(filePath);

  if (path.dirname(targetPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw new Error(
      "Rejected Project document is outside its authorized directory.",
    );
  }

  try {
    await assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(targetPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw new Error("Rejected document is not a regular file.");
    }

    await fs.unlink(targetPath);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") {
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

const TEXT_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "text/plain",
  "text/markdown",
  "text/csv",
  "application/json",
]);

const BINARY_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "application/pdf",
  "application/msword",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
  "application/vnd.ms-powerpoint",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
  "application/vnd.ms-excel",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  "application/zip",
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
]);

const ZIP_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "application/zip",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
]);

async function validateTextContent(filePath, mimeType) {
  const buffer = await fs.readFile(filePath);

  const decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true });

  let content;

  try {
    content = decoder.decode(buffer);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }

  if (content.includes("\0")) {
    return false;
  }

  if (mimeType === "application/json") {
    try {
      JSON.parse(content);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

const OFFICE_PACKAGE_FILES = new Map([
  [
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
    "word/document.xml",
  ],
  [
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
    "ppt/presentation.xml",
  ],
  [
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
    "xl/workbook.xml",
  ],
]);

function validateOfficePackage(filePath, mimeType) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const requiredDocumentFile = OFFICE_PACKAGE_FILES.get(mimeType);

    if (!requiredDocumentFile) {
      resolve(false);
      return;
    }

    yauzl.open(
      filePath,
      {
        lazyEntries: true,
        autoClose: true,
        validateEntrySizes: true,
        strictFileNames: true,
      },
      (error, zipFile) => {
        if (error) {
          resolve(false);
          return;
        }

        const requiredFiles = new Set([
          "[Content_Types].xml",
          "_rels/.rels",
          requiredDocumentFile,
        ]);

        let completed = false;
        let entriesChecked = 0;

        const finish = (result) => {
          if (completed) return;
          completed = true;
          zipFile.close();
          resolve(result);
        };

        zipFile.on("error", () => finish(false));

        zipFile.on("entry", (entry) => {
          entriesChecked += 1;

          if (entriesChecked > 10000) {
            finish(false);
            return;
          }

          requiredFiles.delete(entry.fileName);
          zipFile.readEntry();
        });

        zipFile.on("end", () => {
          finish(requiredFiles.size === 0);
        });

        zipFile.readEntry();
      },
    );
  });
}
async function validateDocumentContent(filePath, mimeType) {
  if (TEXT_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    const detected = await fileTypeFromFile(filePath);

    if (detected) {
      return false;
    }

    return validateTextContent(filePath, mimeType);
  }

  if (!BINARY_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    return false;
  }

  const detected = await fileTypeFromFile(filePath);

  if (!detected) {
    return false;
  }

if (ZIP_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    if (detected.mime !== "application/zip") {
      return false;
    }

    if (mimeType === "application/zip") {
      return true;
    }

    return validateOfficePackage(filePath, mimeType);
  }

  return detected.mime === mimeType;
}

export async function validateProjectDocument(request, response, next) {
  let uploadedPath;

  try {
    uploadedPath = getAuthorizedFilePath(request);

    const userId = request.auth.userId;
    const projectId = request.params.projectId;

    await assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(uploadedPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document is not a regular file.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_INVALID_FILE",
      );
    }

    if (stats.size === 0) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document is empty.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_EMPTY_FILE",
      );
    }

    if (stats.size > MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document exceeds the 20 MiB limit.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE",
        413,
      );
    }

    const uploadedFile = request.file;
    const mimeType = uploadedFile.mimetype;

    if (!ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "Unsupported Project document type.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
      );
    }

    const validContent = await validateDocumentContent(
      uploadedPath,
      mimeType,
    );

    if (!validContent) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document contents do not match its declared type.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_CONTENT_TYPE_MISMATCH",
      );
    }

    request.projectDocumentFile = {
      ...uploadedFile,
      verifiedMimeType: mimeType,
      verifiedExtension: ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_TYPES.get(mimeType).extension,
      verifiedSize: stats.size,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (uploadedPath) {
      try {
        await removeRejectedFile(
          request.auth?.userId,
          request.params?.projectId,
          uploadedPath,
        );
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to clean up rejected Project document:",
          cleanupError,
        );

        return next(
          createValidationError(
            "Project document cleanup failed.",
            "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_CLEANUP_FAILED",
            500,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

  }
}

const TEXT_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "text/plain",
  "text/markdown",
  "text/csv",
  "application/json",
]);

const BINARY_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "application/pdf",
  "application/msword",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
  "application/vnd.ms-powerpoint",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
  "application/vnd.ms-excel",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  "application/zip",
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
]);

const ZIP_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "application/zip",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
]);

async function validateTextContent(filePath, mimeType) {
  const buffer = await fs.readFile(filePath);

  const decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true });

  let content;

  try {
    content = decoder.decode(buffer);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }

  if (content.includes("\0")) {
    return false;
  }

  if (mimeType === "application/json") {
    try {
      JSON.parse(content);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

async function validateDocumentContent(filePath, mimeType) {
  if (TEXT_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    const detected = await fileTypeFromFile(filePath);

    if (detected) {
      return false;
    }

    return validateTextContent(filePath, mimeType);
  }

  if (!BINARY_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    return false;
  }

  const detected = await fileTypeFromFile(filePath);

  if (!detected) {
    return false;
  }

  if (ZIP_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
    return detected.mime === "application/zip";
  }

  return detected.mime === mimeType;
}

export async function validateProjectDocument(request, response, next) {
  let uploadedPath;

  try {
    uploadedPath = getAuthorizedFilePath(request);

    const userId = request.auth.userId;
    const projectId = request.params.projectId;

    await assertSafeProjectDocumentDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(uploadedPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document is not a regular file.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_INVALID_FILE",
      );
    }

    if (stats.size === 0) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document is empty.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_EMPTY_FILE",
      );
    }

    if (stats.size > MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document exceeds the 20 MiB limit.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE",
        413,
      );
    }

    const uploadedFile = request.file;
    const mimeType = uploadedFile.mimetype;

    if (!ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_TYPES.has(mimeType)) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "Unsupported Project document type.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
      );
    }

    const validContent = await validateDocumentContent(
      uploadedPath,
      mimeType,
    );

    if (!validContent) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded document contents do not match its declared type.",
        "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_CONTENT_TYPE_MISMATCH",
      );
    }

    request.projectDocumentFile = {
      ...uploadedFile,
      verifiedMimeType: mimeType,
      verifiedExtension: ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_TYPES.get(mimeType).extension,
      verifiedSize: stats.size,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (uploadedPath) {
      try {
        await removeRejectedFile(
          request.auth?.userId,
          request.params?.projectId,
          uploadedPath,
        );
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to clean up rejected Project document:",
          cleanupError,
        );

        return next(
          createValidationError(
            "Project document cleanup failed.",
            "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_CLEANUP_FAILED",
            500,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: backend\src\middleware\project\projectMediaValidationMiddleware.js
================================================================================
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { fileTypeFromFile } from "file-type";

import {
  ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES,
  MAX_IMAGE_SIZE,
  MAX_VIDEO_SIZE,
} from "./projectMediaUploadMiddleware.js";

import {
  assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory,
  getProjectMediaDirectory,
} from "../../services/Storage/projectMediaStorageService.js";

function createValidationError(message, code, status = 400) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProjectMediaValidationError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = message;

  return error;
}

function getAuthorizedFilePath(request) {
  const userId = String(request.auth?.userId ?? "").trim();
  const projectId = String(request.params?.projectId ?? "").trim();
  const authorizedProjectId = String(
    request.authorizedProject?.id ?? "",
  ).trim();

  if (!userId || !projectId || authorizedProjectId !== projectId) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project media validation is not authorized.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_VALIDATION_UNAUTHORIZED",
      403,
    );
  }

  const uploadedFile = request.file;

  if (!uploadedFile?.path) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Please select a Project media file to upload.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_FILE_REQUIRED",
    );
  }

  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const uploadedPath = path.resolve(uploadedFile.path);

  if (path.dirname(uploadedPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project media file location is invalid.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_INVALID_FILE_LOCATION",
      403,
    );
  }

  return uploadedPath;
}

async function removeRejectedFile(userId, projectId, filePath) {
  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const targetPath = path.resolve(filePath);

  if (path.dirname(targetPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw new Error(
      "Rejected Project media file is outside its authorized directory.",
    );
  }

  try {
    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(targetPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw new Error(
        "Rejected Project media target is not a regular file.",
      );
    }

    await fs.unlink(targetPath);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") {
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

export async function validateProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  let uploadedPath;

  try {
    uploadedPath = getAuthorizedFilePath(request);

    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(
      request.auth.userId,
      request.params.projectId,
    );

    const uploadedFile = request.file;

    // Reject symbolic links and non-regular files before reading content.
    const stats = await fs.lstat(uploadedPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media is not a regular file.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_INVALID_FILE",
      );
    }

    const detectedType = await fileTypeFromFile(uploadedPath);

    if (
      !detectedType ||
      !ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(detectedType.mime)
    ) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded file does not contain a supported media signature.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_CONTENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
      );
    }

    if (detectedType.mime !== uploadedFile.mimetype) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media type does not match its contents.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_TYPE_MISMATCH",
      );
    }

    const maximumSize = detectedType.mime.startsWith("image/")
      ? MAX_IMAGE_SIZE
      : MAX_VIDEO_SIZE;


    if (stats.size > maximumSize) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media exceeds its permitted size.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE",
        413,
      );
    }

    request.projectMediaFile = {
      ...uploadedFile,
      verifiedMimeType: detectedType.mime,
      verifiedExtension: detectedType.ext,
      verifiedSize: stats.size,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (uploadedPath) {
      try {
        await removeRejectedFile(
          request.auth?.userId,
          request.params?.projectId,
          uploadedPath,
        );
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to clean up rejected Project media:",
          cleanupError,
        );

        return next(
          createValidationError(
            "Project media cleanup failed.",
            "PROJECT_MEDIA_CLEANUP_FAILED",
            500,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Project Document Columns
 * =========================================
 */

const PROJECT_DOCUMENT_COLUMNS = `
  id,
  project_id,
  user_id,
  storage_key,
  original_file_name,
  mime_type,
  file_size,
  created_at
`;

/*
 * =========================================
 * Map Database Row
 * =========================================
 */

function mapProjectDocumentRow(row) {
  if (!row) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: row.id,
    projectId: row.project_id,
    storageKey: row.storage_key,
    originalFileName: row.original_file_name,
    mimeType: row.mime_type,
    fileSize: Number(row.file_size),
    createdAt: row.created_at,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Create Project Document Metadata
 * =========================================
 */

export async function createProjectDocument(userId, projectId, document) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("projectId", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .input("storageKey", sql.NVarChar(500), document.storageKey)
    .input(
      "originalFileName",
      sql.NVarChar(255),
      document.originalFileName,
    )
    .input("mimeType", sql.NVarChar(150), document.mimeType)
    .input("fileSize", sql.BigInt, document.fileSize)
    .query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.project_documents (
        project_id,
        user_id,
        storage_key,
        original_file_name,
        mime_type,
        file_size
      )
      OUTPUT
        INSERTED.id,
        INSERTED.project_id,
        INSERTED.user_id,
        INSERTED.storage_key,
        INSERTED.original_file_name,
        INSERTED.mime_type,
        INSERTED.file_size,
        INSERTED.created_at
      VALUES (
        @projectId,
        @userId,
        @storageKey,
        @originalFileName,
        @mimeType,
        @fileSize
      );
    `);

  return mapProjectDocumentRow(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * List Project Document
 * =========================================
 */

export async function listProjectDocuments(userId, projectId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("projectId", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_DOCUMENT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.project_documents
      WHERE user_id = @userId
        AND project_id = @projectId
      ORDER BY created_at DESC, id ASC;
    `);

  return result.recordset.map(mapProjectDocumentRow);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Find Project Document
 * =========================================
 */

export async function findProjectDocumentById(
  userId,
  projectId,
  documentId,
) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("projectId", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .input("documentId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, documentId)
    .query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_DOCUMENT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.project_documents
      WHERE id = @documentId
        AND project_id = @projectId
        AND user_id = @userId;
    `);

  return mapProjectDocumentRow(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Delete Project Document Metadata
 * =========================================
 */

export async function deleteProjectDocument(
  userId,
  projectId,
  documentId,
) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("projectId", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .input("documentId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, documentId)
    .query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.project_documents
      OUTPUT
        DELETED.id,
        DELETED.project_id,
        DELETED.user_id,
        DELETED.storage_key,
        DELETED.original_file_name,
        DELETED.mime_type,
        DELETED.file_size,
        DELETED.created_at
      WHERE id = @documentId
        AND project_id = @projectId
        AND user_id = @userId;
    `);

  return mapProjectDocumentRow(result.recordset[0]);
}

FILE: backend\src\repositories\projectRepository.js

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

const PROJECT_COLUMNS = `
  id,
  user_id,
  title,
  category,
  lifecycle_status,
  record_status,
  is_featured,
  project_data,
  created_at,
  updated_at,
  row_version
`;

function mapProjectRow(row) {
  if (!row) {
    return null;
  }

  const projectData =
    typeof row.project_data === "string"
      ? JSON.parse(row.project_data)
      : row.project_data;

  return {
    ...projectData,
    id: row.id,
    title: row.title,
    category: row.category,
    lifecycleStatus: row.lifecycle_status,
    recordStatus: row.record_status,
    presentation: {
      ...(projectData.presentation || {}),
      isFeatured: Boolean(row.is_featured),
    },
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    rowVersion: Buffer.from(row.row_version).toString("base64"),
  };
}

function bindProjectParameters(request, userId, project) {
  return request
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), project.id)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("title", sql.NVarChar(500), project.title)
    .input("category", sql.NVarChar(100), project.category)
    .input("lifecycleStatus", sql.NVarChar(50), project.lifecycleStatus)
    .input("recordStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), project.recordStatus)
    .input("isFeatured", sql.Bit, Boolean(project.presentation?.isFeatured))
    .input("projectData", sql.NVarChar(sql.MAX), JSON.stringify(project));
}

export async function listProjectsByUser(userId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId).query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE user_id = @userId
      ORDER BY updated_at DESC, id ASC;
    `);

  return result.recordset.map(mapProjectRow);
}

export async function findProjectById(userId, projectId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId).query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE user_id = @userId
        AND id = @id;
    `);

  return mapProjectRow(result.recordset[0]);
}

export async function createProject(userId, project) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  await bindProjectParameters(pool.request(), userId, project).query(`
    INSERT INTO dbo.projects (
      id,
      user_id,
      title,
      category,
      lifecycle_status,
      record_status,
      is_featured,
      project_data
    )
    VALUES (
      @id,
      @userId,
      @title,
      @category,
      @lifecycleStatus,
      @recordStatus,
      @isFeatured,
      @projectData
    );
  `);

  return findProjectById(userId, project.id);
}

export async function updateProject(userId, project, expectedRowVersion) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const request = bindProjectParameters(pool.request(), userId, project).input(
    "expectedRowVersion",
    sql.Binary(8),
    Buffer.from(expectedRowVersion, "base64"),
  );

  const result = await request.query(`
    UPDATE dbo.projects
    SET
      title = @title,
      category = @category,
      lifecycle_status = @lifecycleStatus,
      record_status = @recordStatus,
      is_featured = @isFeatured,
      project_data = @projectData,
      updated_at = SYSUTCDATETIME()
    WHERE id = @id
      AND user_id = @userId
      AND row_version = @expectedRowVersion;
  `);

  if (result.rowsAffected[0] === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  return findProjectById(userId, project.id);
}

export async function deleteProject(userId, projectId, expectedRowVersion) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .input(
      "expectedRowVersion",
      sql.Binary(8),
      Buffer.from(expectedRowVersion, "base64"),
    ).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE id = @id
        AND user_id = @userId
        AND row_version = @expectedRowVersion;
    `);

  return result.rowsAffected[0] > 0;
}

const CATEGORY_VALUES = new Set([
  "software", "web-application", "mobile-application",
  "artificial-intelligence", "data", "cloud-infrastructure",
  "networking", "cybersecurity", "embedded-systems",
  "electronics", "automation", "internet-of-things",
  "research", "design", "business", "academic",
  "community", "other",
]);

const OPTIONS = {
  ownership: ["individual", "team", "client", "organization"],
  source: ["manual", "import", "integration", "ai-assisted"],
  proficiency: ["", "foundational", "intermediate", "advanced", "expert"],
  recognitionType: [
    "", "award", "competition", "grant", "fellowship", "showcase",
    "publication", "presentation", "patent", "media",
    "client-recognition", "academic-recognition", "other",
  ],
  placement: [
    "", "winner", "first-place", "second-place", "third-place",
    "finalist", "semifinalist", "honorable-mention", "selected",
    "recipient", "participant", "other",
  ],
  mediaType: ["image", "video", "diagram"],
  documentType: [
    "report", "presentation", "documentation", "source-file",
    "dataset", "design", "award", "supporting-evidence", "other",
  ],
  visibility: ["public", "private"],
  recordStatus: ["active", "archived"],
};

const TEXT_LIMITS = {
  "organization.name": 200,
  "organization.clientName": 200,
  "organization.website": 1000,
  "location.displayValue": 300,
  "problem.statement": 4000,
  "problem.targetAudience": 1000,
  "problem.context": 3000,
  "problem.importance": 3000,
  "solution.overview": 4000,
  "solution.approach": 4000,
  "solution.architecture": 4000,
  "results.outcome": 4000,
  "results.problemsSolved": 3000,
  "results.impact": 3000,
  "results.beneficiariesAffected": 2000,
  "results.lessonsLearned": 3000,
  "results.futureImprovements": 3000,
  "presentation.shortSummary": 500,
  "presentation.caseStudy": 12000,
  "presentation.featuredMediaId": 200,
  "privateInformation.notes": 5000,
  "sourceContext": 5000 / 10,
};

const COLLECTION_FIELDS = [
  ["problem.constraints", 1500],
  ["problem.objectives", 1500],
  ["solution.features", 1500],
  ["solution.contributions", 1500],
];

function object(value) {
  return value !== null &&
    typeof value === "object" &&
    !Array.isArray(value);
}

function fieldValue(source, path) {
  return path.split(".").reduce(
    (current, key) => object(current) ? current[key] : undefined,
    source,
  );
}

function error(errors, field, code, message) {
  errors.push({ field, code, message });
}

function checkText(errors, field, value, maximum) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maximum) {
    error(
      errors, field, "PROJECT_FIELD_INVALID",
      `This field must be text with at most ${maximum} characters.`,
    );
  }
}

function checkOption(errors, field, value, options) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  if (!options.includes(value)) {
    error(errors, field, "PROJECT_OPTION_INVALID", "Invalid option.");
  }
}

function checkUrl(errors, field, value, maximum = 2000) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  checkText(errors, field, value, maximum);

  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) return;

  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol)) throw new Error();
  } catch {
    error(errors, field, "PROJECT_URL_INVALID", "Invalid HTTP or HTTPS URL.");
  }
}

function checkItems(errors, source, path, callback) {
  const items = fieldValue(source, path);
  if (items === undefined || !Array.isArray(items)) return;

  items.forEach((item, index) => {
    const base = `${path}.${index}`;

    if (!object(item)) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_ITEM_INVALID", "Expected an object.");
      return;
    }

    callback(item, base);
  });
}

function checkOrderedItems(errors, source, path, maximum) {
  checkItems(errors, source, path, (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.id`, item.id, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.text`, item.text, maximum);
  });
}

export function validateNestedProject(source) {
  const errors = [];

  for (const [field, maximum] of Object.entries(TEXT_LIMITS)) {
    checkText(errors, field, fieldValue(source, field), maximum);
  }

  checkOption(errors, "ownership", source.ownership, OPTIONS.ownership);
  checkOption(errors, "source", source.source, OPTIONS.source);

  if (source.modelVersion !== undefined && source.modelVersion !== 2) {
    error(errors, "modelVersion", "PROJECT_VERSION_INVALID",
      "Unsupported Project model version.");
  }

  for (const field of [
    "liveUrl", "repositoryUrl", "documentationUrl",
    "caseStudyUrl", "videoUrl",
  ]) {
    checkUrl(errors, `links.${field}`, source.links?.[field]);
  }

  checkUrl(errors, "organization.website", source.organization?.website, 1000);

  for (const [path, maximum] of COLLECTION_FIELDS) {
    checkOrderedItems(errors, source, path, maximum);
  }

  checkItems(errors, source, "results.metrics", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.label`, item.label, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.value`, item.value, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 500);
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "skillRelationships", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.nameSnapshot`, item.nameSnapshot, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.usageDescription`, item.usageDescription, 1000);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.demonstratedProficiency`,
      item.demonstratedProficiency, OPTIONS.proficiency);

    if (!item.skillId && !item.nameSnapshot) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_SKILL_IDENTITY_REQUIRED",
        "A Skill identifier or snapshot is required.");
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "technologies", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 150);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.category`, item.category, 100);
  });

  const relationshipTypes = [
    ["experienceRelationships", "experienceId"],
    ["educationRelationships", "educationId"],
    ["trainingRelationships", "trainingId"],
    ["certificationRelationships", "certificationId"],
  ];

  for (const [name, idField] of relationshipTypes) {
    checkItems(errors, source, `relatedRecords.${name}`, (item, base) => {
      checkText(errors, `${base}.${idField}`, item[idField], 200);
      checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1000);

      if (!item[idField]) {
        error(errors, `${base}.${idField}`, "PROJECT_RELATIONSHIP_ID_REQUIRED",
          "A related record identifier is required.");
      }

      if (item.snapshot !== undefined && !object(item.snapshot)) {
        error(errors, `${base}.snapshot`, "PROJECT_SNAPSHOT_INVALID",
          "Snapshot must be an object.");
      } else {
        checkText(errors, `${base}.snapshot.name`, item.snapshot?.name, 200);
        checkText(errors, `${base}.snapshot.secondaryLabel`,
          item.snapshot?.secondaryLabel, 200);
      }
    });
  }

  const documentIds = new Set(
    Array.isArray(source.supportingDocuments)
      ? source.supportingDocuments.map((item) => item?.id)
      : [],
  );

  checkItems(errors, source, "awards", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 250);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.organization`, item.organization, 250);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1500);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.recognitionType`,
      item.recognitionType, OPTIONS.recognitionType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.placement`, item.placement, OPTIONS.placement);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.url`, item.url);

    if (item.supportingDocumentIds !== undefined) {
      if (!Array.isArray(item.supportingDocumentIds) ||
          item.supportingDocumentIds.length > 10) {
        error(errors, `${base}.supportingDocumentIds`,
          "PROJECT_AWARD_DOCUMENT_LIMIT", "Maximum 10 document references.");
      } else {
        for (const id of item.supportingDocumentIds) {
          if (!documentIds.has(id)) {
            error(errors, `${base}.supportingDocumentIds`,
              "PROJECT_AWARD_DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND",
              "Referenced document does not exist in this Project.");
          }
        }
      }
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "media", (item, base) => {
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.type`, item.type, OPTIONS.mediaType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.visibility`,
      item.visibility, OPTIONS.visibility);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.status`,
      item.status, OPTIONS.recordStatus);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 255);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.caption`, item.caption, 1000);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.altText`, item.altText, 500);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.externalUrl`, item.externalUrl);

    if (!item.storageKey && !item.externalUrl) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_MEDIA_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
        "A storage key or external URL is required.");
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "supportingDocuments", (item, base) => {
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.documentType`,
      item.documentType, OPTIONS.documentType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.visibility`,
      item.visibility, OPTIONS.visibility);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.status`,
      item.status, OPTIONS.recordStatus);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 255);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1000);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.externalUrl`, item.externalUrl);

    if (!item.storageKey && !item.externalUrl) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
        "A storage key or external URL is required.");
    }
  });

  if (source.visibility !== undefined) {
    if (!object(source.visibility)) {
      error(errors, "visibility", "PROJECT_VISIBILITY_INVALID",
        "Visibility must be an object.");
    } else {
      for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(source.visibility)) {
        if (typeof value !== "boolean") {
          error(errors, `visibility.${name}`, "PROJECT_VISIBILITY_INVALID",
            "Visibility must be true or false.");
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (source.presentation?.featuredMediaId) {
    const featuredId = source.presentation.featuredMediaId;
    const eligible = Array.isArray(source.media) &&
      source.media.some((item) =>
        item?.id === featuredId && item.status !== "archived" &&
        item.visibility !== "private" &&
        (item.storageKey || item.externalUrl),
      );

    if (!eligible) {
      error(errors, "presentation.featuredMediaId",
        "PROJECT_FEATURED_MEDIA_INVALID",
        "Featured media must be active, public, and available.");
    }
  }

  return errors;
}


================================================================================
FILE: backend\src\schemas\projectSchemas.js
================================================================================
import { validateNestedProject } from "./projectNestedValidation.js";

const PROJECT_ID_PATTERN =
  /^project-(?:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}|[0-9]{10,17}-[a-z0-9]{1,20})$/i;

const CATEGORIES = new Set([
  "software",
  "web-application",
  "mobile-application",
  "artificial-intelligence",
  "data",
  "cloud-infrastructure",
  "networking",
  "cybersecurity",
  "embedded-systems",
  "electronics",
  "automation",
  "internet-of-things",
  "research",
  "design",
  "business",
  "academic",
  "community",
  "other",
]);

const LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = new Set([
  "planned",
  "in-progress",
  "completed",
  "maintained",
  "paused",
  "cancelled",
]);

const RECORD_STATUSES = new Set(["active", "archived"]);

const SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS = [
  "userId",
  "user_id",
  "createdAt",
  "updatedAt",
  "rowVersion",
];

function createValidationError(errors) {
  const error = new Error("Project validation failed.");

  error.name = "ProjectValidationError";
  error.code = "PROJECT_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = "Please correct the Project information.";
  error.errors = errors;

  error.fieldErrors = {};

  for (const item of errors) {
    if (!error.fieldErrors[item.field]) {
      error.fieldErrors[item.field] = item.message;
    }
  }

  return error;
}

function addError(errors, field, code, message) {
  errors.push({ field, code, message });
}

function isPlainObject(value) {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function isValidProjectDate(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    return false;
  }

  const match = value.match(
    /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
  );

  if (!match) {
    return false;
  }

  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1;

  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

  return (
    date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    date.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

export function parseProjectInput(value) {
  const errors = [];

  if (!isPlainObject(value)) {
    throw createValidationError([
      {
        field: "project",
        code: "PROJECT_OBJECT_REQUIRED",
        message: "Project data must be an object.",
      },
    ]);
  }

  for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(value, field)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_SERVER_FIELD_FORBIDDEN",
        `${field} cannot be supplied by the client.`,
      );
    }
  }

  if (typeof value.id !== "string" || !PROJECT_ID_PATTERN.test(value.id)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "id",
      "PROJECT_ID_INVALID",
      "A valid Project identifier is required.",
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof value.title !== "string" ||
    !value.title.trim() ||
    value.title.trim().length > 200
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "title",
      "PROJECT_TITLE_INVALID",
      "Project title must contain 1 to 200 characters.",
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof value.role !== "string" ||
    !value.role.trim() ||
    value.role.trim().length > 160
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "role",
      "PROJECT_ROLE_INVALID",
      "Project role must contain 1 to 160 characters.",
    );
  }

  if (!CATEGORIES.has(value.category)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "category",
      "PROJECT_CATEGORY_INVALID",
      "Select a valid Project category.",
    );
  }

  if (!LIFECYCLE_STATUSES.has(value.lifecycleStatus)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "lifecycleStatus",
      "PROJECT_LIFECYCLE_INVALID",
      "Select a valid lifecycle status.",
    );
  }

  if (!RECORD_STATUSES.has(value.recordStatus)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "recordStatus",
      "PROJECT_RECORD_STATUS_INVALID",
      "Select a valid record status.",
    );
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(value.dates)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "dates",
      "PROJECT_DATES_INVALID",
      "Project dates must be an object.",
    );
  } else {
    if (!isValidProjectDate(value.dates.startDate)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.startDate",
        "PROJECT_START_DATE_INVALID",
        "A valid Project start date is required.",
      );
    }

    if (value.dates.endDate && !isValidProjectDate(value.dates.endDate)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_END_DATE_INVALID",
        "Enter a valid Project end date.",
      );
    }

    if (value.dates.isCurrent === true && value.dates.endDate) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_CURRENT_END_DATE_CONFLICT",
        "An ongoing Project cannot have an end date.",
      );
    }

    if (
      value.dates.isCurrent === true &&
      !["in-progress", "maintained"].includes(value.lifecycleStatus)
    ) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "lifecycleStatus",
        "PROJECT_CURRENT_LIFECYCLE_CONFLICT",
        "An ongoing Project must be In Progress or Maintained.",
      );
    }

    if (
      ["completed", "cancelled"].includes(value.lifecycleStatus) &&
      value.dates.isCurrent !== true &&
      !value.dates.endDate
    ) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_END_DATE_REQUIRED",
        "A completion or cancellation date is required.",
      );
    }

    if (
      isValidProjectDate(value.dates.startDate) &&
      isValidProjectDate(value.dates.endDate)
    ) {
      const start = new Date(
        value.dates.startDate.length === 7
          ? `${value.dates.startDate}-01`
          : value.dates.startDate,
      );

      const end = new Date(
        value.dates.endDate.length === 7
          ? `${value.dates.endDate}-01`
          : value.dates.endDate,
      );

      if (end < start) {
        addError(
          errors,
          "dates.endDate",
          "PROJECT_DATE_ORDER_INVALID",
          "End date cannot be earlier than start date.",
        );
      }
    }
  }

  if (
    !isPlainObject(value.presentation) ||
    typeof value.presentation.isFeatured !== "boolean"
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "presentation.isFeatured",
      "PROJECT_FEATURED_INVALID",
      "Project featured status must be a boolean.",
    );
  }

  const urlFields = [
    ["organization.website", value.organization?.website],
    ...[
      "liveUrl",
      "repositoryUrl",
      "documentationUrl",
      "caseStudyUrl",
      "videoUrl",
    ].map((name) => [`links.${name}`, value.links?.[name]]),
  ];

  for (const [field, urlValue] of urlFields) {
    if (urlValue === undefined || urlValue === "") continue;

    let valid = false;

    if (typeof urlValue === "string") {
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(urlValue);
        valid = ["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol);
      } catch {
        valid = false;
      }
    }

    if (!valid) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_URL_INVALID",
        "Enter a valid HTTP or HTTPS URL.",
      );
    }
  }

  const collectionLimits = [
    ["problem.objectives", value.problem?.objectives, 25],
    ["problem.constraints", value.problem?.constraints, 25],
    ["solution.features", value.solution?.features, 40],
    ["solution.contributions", value.solution?.contributions, 40],
    ["results.metrics", value.results?.metrics, 25],
    ["skillRelationships", value.skillRelationships, 60],
    ["technologies", value.technologies, 60],
    ["awards", value.awards, 20],
    ["media", value.media, 30],
    ["supportingDocuments", value.supportingDocuments, 30],
    ...[
      "experienceRelationships",
      "educationRelationships",
      "trainingRelationships",
      "certificationRelationships",
    ].map((name) => [
      `relatedRecords.${name}`,
      value.relatedRecords?.[name],
      50,
    ]),
  ];

  for (const [field, items, maximum] of collectionLimits) {
    if (items === undefined) continue;

    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length > maximum) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_COLLECTION_INVALID",
        `This collection must contain at most ${maximum} items.`,
      );
    }
  }
  errors.push(...validateNestedProject(value));

  if (errors.length > 0) {
    throw createValidationError(errors);
  }

  return structuredClone(value);
}

export function parseProjectRowVersion(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{11}=$/.test(value)) {
    throw createValidationError([
      {
        field: "rowVersion",
        code: "PROJECT_VERSION_INVALID",
        message: "A valid Project version is required.",
      },
    ]);
  }

  return value;
}

FILE: backend\src\services\Service\projectDocumentService.js
